&-\frac{1}{2}W^{-3}\chi{}^{\alpha i}\lambda{}^{\beta}{}_{j}\lambda{}_{\beta i}w{}_{\alpha}{}^{\gamma}\lambda{}_{\gamma}{}^{j} -\frac{i}{8}W^{-3}\varepsilon{}_{i j}X{}^{k l}\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\alpha \beta}\lambda{}_{\beta k}w{}_{\alpha}{}^{\gamma}\lambda{}_{\gamma l}\\

&+\frac{i}{16}W^{-4}\lambda{}^{\alpha i}\lambda{}_{\alpha j}F{}^{a b}\left.W{}_{a b}{}^{\beta j}\right|\lambda{}_{\beta i} +\frac{1}{8}W^{-4}\lambda{}^{\alpha i}\lambda{}_{\alpha j}\lambda{}^{\beta j}\big(\nabla^{a}\nabla_{a}\lambda{}_{\beta i}\big)\\

&+\frac{3}{16}W^{-4}\lambda{}^{\alpha i}\lambda{}_{\alpha j}(\Gamma{}^{a}){}^{\beta \gamma}\lambda{}_{\beta i}\lambda{}_{\gamma}{}^{j}\big(\nabla^{b}w{}_{a b}\big) +W^{-4}\lambda{}^{\alpha i}\lambda{}_{\alpha j}D\lambda{}^{\beta}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\beta}{}^{j}\\
&+W^{-4}\lambda{}^{\alpha i}\lambda{}_{\alpha j}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}^{\beta \gamma}F{}^{a b}\chi{}_{\beta i}\lambda{}_{\gamma}{}^{j} +W^{-4}\lambda{}^{\alpha i}\lambda{}_{\alpha j}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}^{\beta \gamma}F{}^{a b}\chi{}_{\beta}{}^{j}\lambda{}_{\gamma i}\\
&-\frac{3}{256}W^{-4}\lambda{}^{\alpha i}\lambda{}_{\alpha j}w{}^{a b}w{}_{a b}\lambda{}^{\beta}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\beta}{}^{j} -\frac{9}{512}W^{-4}\lambda{}^{\alpha i}\lambda{}_{\alpha j}\varepsilon{}^{a b c d e}(\Gamma{}_{a}){}^{\beta \gamma}w{}_{b c}w{}_{d e}\lambda{}_{\beta i}\lambda{}_{\gamma}{}^{j}\\
&-2W^{-4}\lambda{}^{\alpha i}\lambda{}_{\alpha j}X{}_{i}{}^{j}\chi{}^{\beta k}\lambda{}_{\beta k} -\frac{1}{16}W^{-4}F{}^{\alpha}{}_{\alpha}X{}_{i}{}^{i}F{}^{a b}F{}_{a b}\\
&+\frac{1}{32}W^{-4}\varepsilon{}^{\alpha \beta}\varepsilon{}_{\beta \alpha}X{}^{i}{}_{j}X{}_{i}{}^{j}F{}^{a b}F{}_{a b} -\frac{1}{32}W^{-4}(\Gamma{}^{a}){}^{\alpha}{}_{\alpha}\big(\nabla_{a}W\big)X{}_{i}{}^{i}F{}^{b c}F{}_{b c}\\
&+\frac{i}{32}W^{-4}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\lambda{}_{\alpha k}\varepsilon{}^{i j}(\Gamma{}^{a}){}^{\alpha \beta}\big(\nabla_{a}\lambda{}_{\beta}{}^{k}\big)F{}^{b c}F{}_{b c} -\frac{i}{64}W^{-4}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\lambda{}_{\alpha k}\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\alpha}{}_{\beta}\lambda{}^{\beta k}F{}^{a b}F{}_{a b}\\

&-\frac{i}{64}W^{-4}\lambda{}_{\alpha i}X{}_{j}{}^{i}(\Sigma{}^{a b}){}^{\beta \gamma}(\Gamma{}^{c}){}^{\alpha}{}_{\beta}\big(\nabla_{c}\lambda{}_{\gamma}{}^{j}\big)F{}_{a b} +\frac{i}{64}W^{-4}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}_{i}X{}_{j}{}^{i}(\Sigma{}^{a b}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}(\Gamma{}^{c}){}_{\beta}{}^{\gamma}\big(\nabla_{c}\lambda{}_{\gamma}{}^{j}\big)F{}_{a b}\\
